**Page 154:**

C O N T R A C T O F M E R C E N A RY E M P L OY M E N T
This agreement executed between _____________________________________ (hereafter designated “Employer”) and
____________________________________________ (hereafter designated “Command”) provides employment for the
Command military forces in the service of the Employer. This employment is subject to the terms and conditions outlined below.
I. ASSIGNMENT: The Command is hired for the performance of a mission planned and assigned by the Employer, defined
as a(n) _______________________ contract type under the conventions and usages of contemporary military
terminology. Said Command will perform all operations that fall within the framework of this mission, as well as serving
the general interests and needs of the Employer.
II. FORCES: The Command agrees to provide combat and support forces, estimated at __________ units of combat and/
or combat support troops, as of the date this contract goes into effect. The Employer reserves the right to terminate the
agreement if actual forces mustered at the time this contract goes into effect are less than two-thirds of the originally
estimated forces or if they exceed the agreed-upon strength by more than 10 percent, unless excess troops serve for no
more money than originally designated in this agreement.
III. LENGTH OF SERVICE: This contract will remain in effect for ______________, commencing on ___________
and concluding on _______________.
On the commencement date, the Command agrees to be located on ______________. The Employer agrees to
pay ______ percent of the transportation costs incurred by the Command to reach the location. Upon termination of
the agreement, the Command will be discharged from all duties and responsibilities to the Employer, unless discharge is
superseded by a new agreement.
IV. REMUNERATION: The Employer agrees to pay ___________________ per month to the Command. This money
is to be held in escrow agreed upon by both parties. Combat bonuses based on this amount will be paid for engaging
in combat (one-half), successfully completing the defined objectives of the combat (one-half), and for exemplary service
(another one-half) for a maximum combat bonus of one and a half times the monthly remuneration.
V. SUPPORT: The Employer agrees to provide __________ percent of the logistical support of the Command. The
Employer will reimburse the Command for the value if the Command provides its own supplies. Resupply of munitions
and other specific battlefield matériel after each major battle or campaign shall consist of __________ percent over and
above said supply requirements.
VI. TRANSPORTATION: The Employer agrees to provide for __________ percent of the interstellar and/or
interplanetary transport of the Command. The Employer shall pay the Command any standard and customary costs to
reimburse the Command for providing transport for the Unit or any part thereof.
VII. SALVAGE RIGHTS: All enemy combat equipment recovered during the operation of this contract shall be divided
between the Employer and the Command, with the unit receiving __________ percent of the estimated market value of
the equipment.
VIII. RIGHTS: The Unit hereby agrees to structures of the following nature:
___________ __________ __________ __________ ____________________________________ _________
The Command is guaranteed to retain internal coherence and consistency of its structure within the usual limits of this
assigned status.
IX. BATTLEFIELD CONDUCT: The Command agrees to battlefield conduct as set forth by the Ares Conventions.
Any violations of said conventions are solely the responsibility of the Command and render this contract and further
payments null and void.
X. OTHER TERMS: Other terms of this contract, agreed to by both parties, shall be negotiated on a case-by-case basis,
individual signed and witnessed, as riders to this document.
Signed at _______________________________ on this ______ day of ______ , in the year _________________
Unit Commander _______________________________________________________________________
Signed at _______________________________ on this ______ day of ______ , in the year ___________
Employer _____________________________________________________________________________
Witnessed by: _________________________________________________________________________
